שָׂא נָא כֵלֶיךָ · sa na khele-kha · 'Please take your gear'

DERIVATION RECORD — freeze-era draft note: DRAFT 2026-08-06 · RELAY #10–#11 · first NEW derivation post-amendment wave. SPAN RULING R1 (owner/Claude): gen_46 = Genesis 27:1–40; gen_47 = 27:41–28:9 (derived later as its own unit). Piece plan: ten pieces of four verses (1–4 · 5–8 · 9–13 · 14–17 · 18–22 · 23–26 · 27–29 · 30–33 · 34–37 · 38–40). Full bar C. SNAPSHOT + debut_map. verse_trees OMITTED [bar C.7]. Elisions … . §1a Claude law. Pieces 1–7 VERIFIED (RELAY #11–#17). Piece 8 BATCH AUTHORIZED 27:30–40 (owner wide-batch); STOP for whole-unit review. Onkelos BUFFER PENDING. No freeze until Claude whole-unit green. Draft scenario checks = dry-run only. ===================================================================== GOVERNING WEIGHS (Claude care-points — unit-wide; file before any verse) ===================================================================== (1) CENTERPIECE — DEMANDEE-MISMATCH MACHINE. Isaac's hunt-chain at 27:3–4 (five 2ms volitives in one breath, one addressee: sa / ve-tze / ve-tzuda / va-ase / ve-havia + purpose-cohortative ve-okhela) rides ONE compound demand per the frozen compound class (gen_41 qach_va_lekh; gen_40 multi-imperative single-frame) — pushed on ESAU alone. Jacob later performs the content (food brought, 27:14ff) but is NOT the demandee: by the machine's own addressee standard the compound can NEVER pop — the blessing is obtained over an unpaid demand, and the SPECS queue itself exhibits the deception. Encode letter-faithfully and say it loudly. Label: LET(sa_tze_tzuda_ase_havia(esav)). (2) FENCE at 27:6–7 (later pieces): Rivqah reporting Isaac's speech = FACTS (Excerpt A retelling fence); her own live shema be-qoli / lekh / qach (27:8–9, 13) = real pushes on Jacob; his 27:14 va-yelekh va-yiqach is same-root double performance — weigh pops with contingent labels continuing after #23-or-#24. (3) BLESS-EVENTS ASYMMETRY (census with total care across unit): narrated bless-EVENT (va-yevarakhehu, wayyiqtol) fires for Jacob; Esau's two barakhe-ni ("bless me, me also!") pushes at 27:34/38 are answered in SPEECH-content only — check whether the letter EVER narrates a bless-event on Esau in-span; if not, both demands stay OPEN and the withholding is the letter's own. (4) BLESSING JUSSIVES (27:28–29, later): ve-yiten ha-elohim ("may God give") = demand-on-God class per gen_32 fence, OPEN; yaavdu-kha third-party jussives = LET pushes; heve gevir ("be master") = benediction-imperative per gen_41 hayi precedent (push; content-alternative staged). (5) FALSE-IDENTITY FACTS (later pieces): "I am Esau your firstborn" (27:19) and the ani of 27:24 — the machine records speech, never adjudicates truth; hold as spoken facts; flag collision with gen_43 REGISTRY (names are written; the mouth misuses one). (6) CAREERS TO LAND: bekhorah token 6 at 27:36 citing gen_43's machine; tzayid 6718 toks 5–11 (chase/hunt noun — not yet in 27:1–4; starts 27:5); voice/hands pair 27:22; mirma ("deceit") debut; charadah hapax; trembling + great bitter cry (tzeaqah tok3 after Sodom's two). Piece-1 careers: matamim 4303 tok1; teli 8522 hapax; tzud 6679 tok1; tzedah/tzayid-food 6720 tok1–2 (KTIV pair); ahav 157 tok5; barakh 1288 tok34 (imperfect purpose, not narrated EVENT). (7) TESTS asleep (27:9's goats attribute-fenced when reached; no instrument fire in 27:1–4). REGISTRY expected silent — confirm per piece. CROWNS (chain-attested + DB-verified). THE LEAN VOICE OF JACOB [G46-01, Minchat Shai on 27:22; Bereshit Rabbah 65]: הקל קול יעקב ("THE VOICE is the voice of Jacob, but the hands are Esau's hands") — the sentence's first voice-word written DEFECTIVE and UNIQUE in the whole Torah (one lean the-voice token), while its own echo in the same breath runs full: the midrash rides exactly this — when Jacob's voice goes lean in the synagogues and study-houses, Esau's hands prevail. THE HUNT'S EXTRA LETTER [G46-02, Minchat Shai on 27:3, the Ramah; Kitzur]: צידה ("hunt me GAME") — WRITTEN with a closing heh, READ without it (both tokens side by side on SNAPSHOT): the Ramah's one extra-heh of its kind in the Torah; the Kitzur reads the surplus five-letter as the FIVE laws of slaughter Isaac taught with the hunt. THE BOW-VERBS, ONE LEAN ONE FULL [G46-03, Minchat Shai on 27:29, the Ramah's Masorah]: וישתחו לך לאמים ("peoples shall BOW to you") — written one closing vav, read doubled (a written/read token pair), while the verse's second bow-verb is written with both vavs: the lean and the full side by side in the blessing (the Kitzur counts the lean vav toward Edom's six served generations). THE BLESS-CAREER COMPLETED [G46-04/05, Minchat Shai on 27:29, the Ramah's three-list]: ומברכיך ("and THOSE WHO BLESS YOU — blessed") — plene with the yod, the middle station between Abraham's call (מברכיך, 12:3) and Balaam's mouth (Num 24:9): the whole three-token career plene, letter-exact (run 4's vocalization-law crown gains its center link). THE PAUSE THAT SAVES THE SENTENCE [G46-06, Minchat Shai on 27:19; the Zohar; Bereshit Rabbah 65:16]: אנכי עשו בכרך ("I am... Esau your firstborn") — the tradition cuts Jacob's words at אנכי ("I am who I am; Esau is your firstborn"; R. Levi: "I am — destined to receive the Ten Utterances"), and SNAPSHOT's own accent carries the cut: אנכי under pashta (the postpositive disjunctive) — the saving pause is in the letter's music. THE TWO PRESSED BLESS-ME'S [G46-08, Minchat Shai on 27:19 + 27:31]: תברכני ("that your soul may BLESS ME") — the nun dageshed as ruled, and the same pressed nun returns in Esau's mouth (27:31, builder-asserted): the disguised request and the desperate one pointed identically. THE CRY THAT RETURNS [G46-15, Minchat Shai on 27:34, the Masorah's mirror-set]: ויצעק צעקה ("Esau CRIED an exceedingly great and bitter CRY") — the doubled cry-figure once in the Torah (verified by adjacency); the mirror is Mordechai's great and bitter cry (Esth 4:1; edge): the extorted cry repaid on the decree-day — the two bitter cries filed as one pair. THE DECEIT REPAID IN KIND [G46-16, Kitzur on 27:35]: במרמה ("your brother came WITH DECEIT") — exactly twice in the Torah: Jacob's deceit here, his own sons' answer at Shechem (34:13) — the word returns on the deceiver's house in a two-token lock. THE BLESSING'S LADDER [G46-17, Kitzur on 27:36]: ברכתי ("my blessing") — four consonantal tokens: Esau's "he TOOK my blessing" (27:36), Jacob's restitution "TAKE, please, my blessing" to Esau's face (33:11), God's "I will COMMAND my blessing" (Lev 25:21) — taken, returned, commanded; the fourth is the verb at Ishmael's promise (17:20, homograph named). THE TWO-WAY SERVE-VERB AGAIN [G46-18, Minchat Shai on 27:40; Bereshit Rabbah 67]: ואת אחיך תעבד ("your brother you shall SERVE") — defective, and the whole family lean: תעבד twelve Torah tokens, no plene (builder-asserted), from Cain's soil to the labor-commandment; the midrash reads the reversal in the missing vav exactly as at the twins' birth (frozen G43-03). THE TEN-WORD DEW-BLESSING AND THE NAME-COUNT [G46-19/20, Kitzur on 27:28-29]: ויתן לך — the dew-verse counts exactly TEN words (machine-checked; against the Ten Utterances), and the whole blessing TWENTY-SIX read words (ten plus sixteen, the written/read bow-pair counted once, builder-asserted): the blessing carries the four-letter Name's own number. THE SWORD AT THE BOOK'S MIDDLE [G46-23, Minchat Shai on 27:40, manual]: ועל חרבך תחיה ("by your sword you shall live") — the Masorah's half-of-Genesis marker (its verse-total: 1,534); SNAPSHOT's versification counts 1,533 with 27:40 standing 768th — one seam off the arithmetic middle: the verse-count split between the Masorah's total and the codex line is itself the finding (variance, named). SMALLER CROWNS [G46-07/10/11/12/13/14/21/22]: מצידי ("of my game," 27:19) plene against the old correction-book; מראת ("dimmed from SEEING," 27:1) unique and lean; כמתעתע ("as a mocker," 27:12) unique, first tav soft; the hairy brother's two lean tokens (שער 27:11, שערת 27:23) — SNAPSHOT with Minchat Kohen's retraction, the goat-reading four-list a named dispute; ואברכהו ("and I BLESSED him," 27:33) with the vav in qamatz (the past-converter) against its future-pointed pair at Abraham's verse (Isa 51:2; edge — the Kitzur: the blessing of Abraham passes to Jacob); מי אפוא ("WHO then...?") — initials ו-מ-א, the letters of אמו ("his mother"): the Kitzur answers Isaac's question from the verse's own openings; הוה ("BE lord," 27:29) unique (the snow-command its pair, Job 37:6; edge); ולכה ("and FOR YOU then, what shall I do, my son?," 27:37) — the Torah's only heh-written dative (the two consonantal look-alikes are Balaam's go-imperatives, Num 22:17, 23:7). DISCREPANCY [G46-09, Minchat Shai on 27:27/34/41]: the chapter's four ruled bless-tokens — ויברכהו and ברכו (27:27), ברכני (27:34), ברכו (27:41) — all wanted with the resh in plain sheva "per our custom"; SNAPSHOT points HATAF PATACH at every one (builder-asserted): the sheva-vs-hataf split now counts EIGHT stations, all resh (gen_45's boundary result stands: the ayin stays bare). Watchlist. INSTRUMENT GAPS: קצתי ("I am weary of my life," 27:46) carries the SMALL QOF — letter-size layer uninstrumented (the Kitzur's temple-height reading named); the pause-readings at 27:19 ride the accent layer (instrumented, claimed above). READINGS (named-only, unclaimed): the Kitzur's Passover frame (וירח = "it was the day of Pesach"; the two kids — one for the feast, one for the offering; the נאה לך initials-play at 27:9); the four-exiles reading of the hunting-gear (27:3); the letter-count reading at 27:33 (gehinnom, "the netherworld," entering with Esau); Isaac's trembling read against the Rebbi-and-Antoninus table; the firstborn-pair בכרך (27:19 with Exod 4:23 — "Israel is My firstborn... I slay YOUR firstborn," the Kitzur's two-edged pair, named).

# וְאֶעֱשֶׂה אֹתָם מַטְעַמִּים לְאָבִיךָ כַּאֲשֶׁר אָהֵב
# "[EN-AID] Go now to the flock and take for me from there two good kids of
# the goats, and I will make them delicacies for your father as he loves."

# ‹לֶךְ … וְקַח› (“go … and-take”) — rivqah speaks a demand — LET: go-take-
# come/bring(Jacob)
m.declare("rivqah", "LET",
          "lekh_qach_heveta(yaaqov)")
# ‹גְּדָיֵי עִזִּים טֹבִים … וְאֶעֱשֶׂה … כַּאֲשֶׁר אָהֵב› (“young-goat she-
# goat good … and-make … like-as/which have-affection-for”) — fact holds:
# good-attribute-fenced-kids; rivqah-will-make-delicacy
m.fact("tovim_attribute_fenced_kids",
       "rivqah_will_make_matamim")
